Results · Definitions

← suite simplipy

sp-acj4-165-complex contingent

category simplipy-complex-branch

Summary

Given
\(x \in \mathbb{C},\quad x \neq i,\quad x \neq -i,\quad x \neq 0,\quad \frac{1}{x} \neq i,\quad \frac{1}{x} \neq -i\)
Claim
\(\displaystyle \arcsin\left(\cos\left(\arctan x\right)\right) \overset{?}{=} \left|\arctan\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\right|\)
Expected
contingent true at \(x = 1\); false at \(x = 1 + i\)

equal at some admissible points, different at others

witness (true): \(x = 1\)

witness (false): \(x = 1 + i\)

SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[165]: asin cos atan _0 -> abs atan inv _0

Lean proof: included

Source (complex-branch.sexp)

(case sp-acj4-165-complex (category simplipy-complex-branch)
  (note "SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[165]: asin cos atan _0  ->  abs atan inv _0")
  (var x complex)
  (assume (!= x i))
  (assume (!= x (- i)))
  (assume (nonzero x))
  (assume (!= (/ x) i))
  (assume (!= (/ x) (- i)))
  (ask (= (asin (cos (atan x))) (abs (atan (/ x)))))

witness true at x = 1
⊢1 ≠ i
∧1 ≠ −i
∧nonzero(1)
∧1 / 1 ≠ i
∧1 / 1 ≠ −i
∧asin(cos(atan(1))) = |atan(1 / 1)|
definedness side conditions (2, and 4 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
⊢nonzero(1)↓ trivially true
⊢atan(1)↓
⊢cos(atan(1))↓ trivially true
⊢asin(cos(atan(1)))↓ trivially true
⊢atan(1 / 1)↓
⊢|atan(1 / 1)|↓ trivially true

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceasin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))→-Abs(atan(1/x)) + asin(1/sqrt(x**2 + 1))
sympy_extras.assumptions.askEq(Residual, 0)→None

Machines: pi5 (indicative): it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise (Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled).

import sympy

import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.trigsimp(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(satsimp.refine(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.positive(W), strict=True))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))

print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atan(1/x)) + asin(1/sqrt(x**2 + 1))
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceasin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))→-Abs(atan(1/x)) + asin(1/sqrt(x**2 + 1))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atan(1/x)) + asin(1/sqrt(x**2 + 1))
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satsimp.refinesatsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atan(1/x)) + asin(1/sqrt(x**2 + 1))

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False)

# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
Result = refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
